with other logics for coinductively defined relations based on similarity, previously defined in the literature.

First we start with HML (Definition
3
), characterising bisimulation equivalence
[
25
]
,
that
determines a clear upper bound on the expressiveness of the logics for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
. In what follows, (strict) inclusions and
equalities between logics are up to logical equivalence. For example,
{\mathcal{L}}\subseteq{\mathcal{L}^{\prime}}

means
that, for each
\varphi\in{\mathcal{L}}
, there is some
\varphi^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{L}^{\prime}}
that is logically equivalent to

\varphi
.

Proposition 9
.

\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}\subset\mbox{HML}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}\subset\mbox{HML}
.

Proof.

The only non-standard operator used in
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
is the

\langle\!|\cdot|\!\rangle
operator (
\llbracket\cdot\rrbracket
in
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
,
respectively), but, as observed earlier, formulae

\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi
(
\llbracket a?\rrbracket\varphi
, respectively) can be
expressed in
HML
as
\langle a?\rangle\varphi\vee[\,a?\,]\mathbf{ff}

(
\langle a?\rangle\mathbf{tt}\wedge[\,a?\,]\varphi
, respectively). We assume
no distinction between input and output actions in
HML
. As noted
before in Remark
3
, the HML formula
\langle a?\rangle\mathbf{tt}

cannot be expressed in
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
and thus
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
cannot express
its dual
[\,a?\,]\mathbf{ff}
.
∎

The simulation and ready simulation preorders
[
11
]
have been logically characterised in the
literature using subsets of
HML
, see for instance
[
12
,
15
,
18
]
.

Definition 9
.

The logics for plain simulation and ready simulation, denoted by
\mathcal{L}_{s}
and
\mathcal{L}_{rs}
respectively,
are defined by the following BNF grammars, where
a\in L
:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{s}&:&\phi::=\mathbf{ff}\mid\mathbf{tt}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\langle a\rangle\phi.\\
\mathcal{L}_{rs}&:&\phi::=\mathbf{ff}\mid\mathbf{tt}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\langle a\rangle\phi\mid[\,a\,]\mathbf{ff}.\end{array}

Remark 5
.

In the classic literature, see for instance
[
11
,
12
,
18
]
,
the logics characterising simulation and ready simulation include
neither the disjunction operator
\vee
nor the constant
\mathbf{ff}
;
however, as was already proved in
[
15
]
, those constructs can
be safely added to those logics without altering their discriminating
power.

Proposition 10
.

For the logics
\mathcal{L}_{s},\mathcal{L}_{rs}
and
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
the following properties hold:

1.

\mathcal{L}_{rs}\supseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
,

2.

\mathcal{L}_{rs}\not\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
,

3.

\mathcal{L}_{s}\not\supseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
and

4.

\mathcal{L}_{s}\not\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
.

Proof.

Let us consider a state
p
with transitions
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
,
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
and
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
, and state

q
with the transitions
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q
and
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q
. Observe that
p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
and therefore

\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(p)\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(q)
(Theorem
2
). This will be used in the proofs of statements 2–4 below.
We consider each statement separately.

1.

\mathcal{L}_{rs}\supseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. All formulas in
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
are also in
\mathcal{L}_{rs}
.

2.

\mathcal{L}_{rs}\not\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. Considering states
p
and
q
above, we
have that
p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
and therefore
p\leq_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}}q
. However, the formula
\langle b?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
in

\mathcal{L}_{rs}
is satisfied by
p
and not
q
. So there is no
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
formula equivalent to it.

3.

\mathcal{L}_{s}\not\supseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. Considering states
p
and
q
above, we have that

q
is simulated by
p
, that is
q\leq_{\mathcal{L}_{s}}p
, but formula
\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}
is satisfied by

q
and not by
p
and therefore
q\not\leq_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}}p
. It follows that there is no formula
in
\mathcal{L}_{s}
that is logically equivalent to
\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}
.

4.

\mathcal{L}_{s}\not\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. Considering again states
p
and
q
. We have that

p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
and therefore
p\leq_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}}q
, but
p\not\leq_{\mathcal{L}_{s}}q
because
p

satisfies
\langle a?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
but
q
does not. This means that
\langle a?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
cannot be expressed in

\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
up to logical equivalence. ∎

As we already discussed in Remark
5
, disjunction
does not add any distinguishing power to
\mathcal{L}_{rs}
. However,
disjunction is needed for the validity of statement
1
in
Proposition
10
, as the next lemma formalises.

Lemma 11
.

Let
\mathcal{L}_{rs}^{\prime}
be defined as

\mathcal{L}_{rs}^{\prime}:\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\langle a\rangle\phi\mid[\,a\,]\mathbf{ff},

where
a\in L
. Then, there is no formula in
\mathcal{L}_{rs}^{\prime}
such that is logically equivalent to the formula

\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
.

Proof.

Let
\phi
be a formula in
\mathcal{L}_{rs}^{\prime}
. We will show that
\phi
is not equivalent to

\psi=\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
. To this end, note first of all that, up to logical equivalence,
\phi
can be
written as
\phi=\bigwedge_{i\in I}\langle a_{i}\rangle\phi_{i}\wedge\bigwedge_{j\in J}[\,b_{j}\,]\mathbf{ff}
, for finite sets
I

and
J
, actions
a_{i}
and
b_{j}
and formulae
\phi_{i}
. Notice that if
I=J=\emptyset
, then
\phi
is a
tautology which is not logically equivalent to
\psi=\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
. Let us assume then that
I\neq\emptyset
or
J\neq\emptyset
. We distinguish two cases.

First, assume that some
b_{j}
is different from
a?
. Let us consider
the process
p
such that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{j}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{j}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{j}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{j}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
and
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
. We
have that
p\models\psi
but
p\not\models\phi
, which implies that

\psi
and
\phi
are not logically equivalent.

Next, assume that all the
b_{j}
’s are equal to
a?
. There are two subcases.

•

J\neq\emptyset
. In this case, if there exists some

i\in I
such that
a_{i}=a?
,
\phi
is unsatisfiable, and
thus is not equivalent to
\psi
. Otherwise, let us consider
two subcases. If
I=\emptyset
,
\phi
is logically
equivalent to
[\,a?\,]\mathbf{ff}
which is not equivalent to

\psi
. Otherwise, if
I\neq\emptyset
, the process
p
such
that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
and
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
, clearly satisfies

\psi
but
p\not\models\phi
.

•

J=\emptyset
. In this case,
I\neq\emptyset
. There are two cases: either there exists some

i\in I
such that
a_{i}=a?
, or there is not. In the former, the process
\text{nil}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\text{nil}
satisfies
\psi
but does not satisfy
\phi
. In
the latter, let us consider a process
q
such that
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q
and
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q
. Clearly
q\models\psi
but

q\not\models\phi
, which implies that those formulae are not equivalent.∎

Besides the classic semantic relations (bisimulation, simulation and
ready simulation) there is a less standard relation in the literature,
the so-called covariant-contravariant simulation, that is quite close
to
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
in its formulation. Let us recall here this relation and
its characterising logic.

Definition 10

(Covariant-contravariant simulation
[
16
]
)
.

Consider an alphabet
A
, and let
\{A^{r},A^{l},A^{\mathit{bi}}\}
be a partition of this alphabet. A binary
relation
R
over states in an LTS is a covariant-contravariant simulation relation if and only if for each

(p,q)\in R
the following conditions hold:

•

for all
a\in A^{r}\cup A^{\mathit{bi}}
and all
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
there exists
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
with
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
,

•

for all
b\in A^{l}\cup A^{\mathit{bi}}
, and all
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
there exists
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
with
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
.

We will write
p\lesssim_{CC}q
if there exists a covariant-contravariant simulation
R
such that
(p,q)\in R
.

Let us note that the case of
A^{l}=A^{\mathit{bi}}=\emptyset
and
A^{r}=L
would yield plain simulation. In
addition, the case
A^{l}=A^{r}=\emptyset
and
A^{\mathit{bi}}=L
would yield bisimulation.
Covariant-contravariant simulation are also called
XY
-simulation (for instance in
[
1
]
). There the
sets
X
and
Y
might not be disjoint, but the definitions are equivalent.

Definition 11

(Covariant-contravariant logic
[
16
]
)
.

The syntax of the logic for covariant-contravariant, denoted by
\mathcal{L}_{CC}
, is defined by the following BNF grammar:.

\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\langle a\rangle\phi\mid[\,b\,]\phi,

where
a\in A^{r}\cup A^{\mathit{bi}}
and
b\in A^{l}\cup A^{\mathit{bi}}
.

There are clear similarities between Definition
10

and the definition of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
in Definition
2
.
Nevertheless, as it is not difficult to prove from the characterising
logics, the relations
\lesssim_{CC}
and
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
are not related.

Proposition 12
.

The covariant-contravariant simulation and
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
are not comparable.

Proof.

Given that for the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
relation the input and output sets are disjoint, we consider

A^{\mathit{bi}}=\emptyset
. Let us consider the following processes:

•

,

and
s\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}s
.

We discuss the different alternatives:

•

If
A^{r}=I
,
A^{l}=O
, then
\mathcal{L}_{CC}
can be expressed as

\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\langle a?\rangle\phi\mid[\,b!\,]\phi,

which is not comparable with
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}

\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\llbracket a?\rrbracket\phi\mid[\,b!\,]\phi.

First, let us consider the processes
p
and
q
.
p\lesssim_{CC}q
which implies that

\mathcal{L}_{CC}(p)\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{CC}(q)
, but
p\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
whereas
q\not\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
. It follows that there is no formula in
\mathcal{L}_{CC}
that is logically equivalent to

\llbracket a?\rrbracket[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
.

Analogously, consider
r
and
s
. Is clear that
r\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}s
which implies that

\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(r)\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(s)
, but
r\models\langle b?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
and
s\not\models\langle b?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
, which
implies that there is no formula in
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
that is logically equivalent to
\langle b?\rangle\mathbf{tt}
.

•

If
A^{r}=O
,
A^{l}=I
, then
\mathcal{L}_{CC}
can be expressed as

\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid[\,a?\,]\phi\mid\langle b!\rangle\phi,

that is not comparable to
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}

\phi::=\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\phi\mid\langle b!\rangle\phi.

First, let us consider
p
and
q
. Observe that
p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
, which implies

\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(p)\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}(q)
. Now,
p\models[\,a?\,]\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
but
q\not\models[\,a?\,]\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
. It follows that there is no formula in
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}}
that is logically equivalent to

[\,a?\,]\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
.

Analogously, consider
r
and
s
. Is clear that
s\lesssim_{CC}r
which implies that

\mathcal{L}_{CC}(s)\subseteq\mathcal{L}_{CC}(r)
, but
s\models\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
and

r\not\models\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
, which implies that there is no formula in
\mathcal{L}_{CC}
that is
logically equivalent to
\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle[\,\delta!\,]\mathbf{ff}
.∎

The comparison between the logics characterising the relations

\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
and Tretmans’
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}
is of particular interest. We will delay
this comparison till Section
6
, as we will make
use of some of the results in the following sections.

4
Adding fixed points to the logics

Even if an LTS is image-finite and has a finite number of states, it can capture infinite behaviours, for example by
means of loops. As is well known, the logics presented in Section
3
can only describe properties of finite
fragments of process computations and can be made more expressive by extending them with fixed-point operators.

Here we only explicitly show how to add the greatest fixed-point
operator to one of the characterising logics for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
,
presented in Section
3
. We will make use of this
extended logic in Section
5
to define the
characteristic formula for processes with respect to
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
.

In this section we follow the approach of the so-called equational
\mu
-calculus (see, for example,

[
4
,
30
]
to which we refer the reader for further details). We assume a countably infinite set

\mathsf{Var}
of formula variables. The meaning of formula variables is specified by means of a declaration. A
declaration is a function
D:\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
that associates a formula
D(X)
with each variable

X
. Intuitively, if
D(X)=\phi
, then
X
stands for the largest solution of the equation
X=\phi
. In what follows,
we are only interested in the restriction of a declaration to a finite collection of formula variables, and we express such
a declaration as a system of equations
X_{1}=\phi_{1},\ldots,X_{n}=\phi_{n}
, with
n\geq 1
.

We interpret the language over the set
S
of processes in some
LTS. Since a formula
\varphi
may contain formula variables, its
semantics—that is, the set of processes in
S
that satisfy

\varphi
—is defined relative to an environment

\sigma:\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(S)
. Intuitively,
\sigma

assigns to each variable the set of processes in
S
for which the
variable holds true.

As is well known, the set of all environments
[\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(S)]
is a complete lattice with respect to the partial
order induced by pointwise set inclusion.

Definition 12
.

The syntax of the logic for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
with greatest fixed points,
denoted by
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
, is defined by the following BNF grammar

\phi::=X\mid\mathbf{tt}\mid\mathbf{ff}\mid\phi\wedge\phi\mid\phi\vee\phi\mid\llbracket a?\rrbracket\phi\mid[\,a!\,]\phi,

where
a?\in I
,
a!\in O
and
X\in\mathsf{Var}
.

The semantics of
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
is given by:

•

(\sigma,p)\models X
iff
p\in\sigma(X)
.

•

(\sigma,p)\models\mathbf{tt}
for all
p
.

•

(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{1}\wedge\varphi_{2}
iff
(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{1}
and
(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{2}
.

•

(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{1}\vee\varphi_{2}
iff
(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{1}
or
(\sigma,p)\models\varphi_{2}
.

•

(\sigma,p)\models[\,a!\,]\phi
iff
(\sigma,p^{\prime})\models\phi
for each
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
.

•

(\sigma,p)\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket\phi
iff
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
and
(\sigma,p^{\prime})\models\phi
,
for each
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
.

The semantics of each formula
\varphi
is therefore the function

\llparenthesis\varphi\rrparenthesis:[\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(S)]\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(S)
defined thus:

\llparenthesis\varphi\rrparenthesis\sigma=\{p\mid(\sigma,p)\models\varphi\}.

A declaration function
D
induces an endofunction
\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis
over the complete lattice
[\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(S)]
defined by

(\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis\sigma)(X)=\llparenthesis D(X)\rrparenthesis\sigma.

Proposition 13
.

The function
\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis
is monotone. Indeed, so is
\llparenthesis\varphi\rrparenthesis

for each formula
\varphi
.

Proof.

Since
\llbracket a?\rrbracket\varphi
is logically equivalent to the HML formula
\langle a?\rangle\mathbf{tt}\wedge[\,a?\,]\varphi
, the result
follows straightforwardly from the monotonicity of the semantic counterparts of the operators in HML (see

[
30
]
, for example).
∎

Since
\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis
is a monotone endofunction over a complete lattice, it
has a greatest (and a least) fixed point
\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis_{\max}
by the
Knaster-Tarski Theorem
[
40
]
.

Theorem 14
.

For all states
i,s
in some LTS,

i\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}s\quad\mbox{iff}\quad\forall\phi\in\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}\quad{s\models\phi}\Rightarrow{i\models\phi}.

Proof.

Since
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
is a sublogic of
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
, the
if
implication of the proof follows by
Theorem
4
. For the
only if
implication,
the proof follows by applying the standard techniques for
\mu\mbox{-calculus}
and
bisimulation equivalence (see, for example,
[
38
, Section
5.4
]
).
∎

Remark 6
.

We showed in Section
3
how
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
are dual logics (Corollary
6
). Analogously to the construction of
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
from
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
, we could add
\mu
, the least fixed point operator, to the logic
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
, for defining
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\mu}
.

Logics like
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
can be used in model checking in order to
evaluate if an implementation satisfies some desired properties. There
is already a tool support for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
implemented in the
mCRL2
tool
set
[
14
,
22
,
24
]
, including a minimisation algorithm. This
allows one to model check
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
using the
mCRL2
tool. In fact, the
built-in logic used in
mCRL2
is the
first-order modal

\mu
-calculus

[
27
]
, which is an extension of the

\mu
-calculus where the actions are allowed to include data
parameters. We omit that extension here, but we show some examples of
properties that can be expressed with our logic.

Example 2
.

Let us illustrate some useful properties that can be expressed using the logic
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}^{\nu}
.

•

In the theory of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}
, implementations are supposed to
be input enabled, that is, each of their reachable states
should be able to perform every input action. This
requirement can be expressed as follows:

X=\bigwedge_{a?\in I}\llbracket a?\rrbracket X\wedge\bigwedge_{b!\in O}[\,b!\,]X.

•

The next formula formalises that a property
\varphi

must hold invariantly over input-enabled systems:

X=\varphi\wedge(\bigwedge_{a?\in I}\llbracket a?\rrbracket X\wedge\bigwedge_{o!\in O}[\,o!\,]X).

That is,
\varphi
must be true, and must remain true after performing any input or output action.

5
Characteristic formulae

Once we have logically characterised
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
, it is natural to search
for a single logical formula that characterises completely the
behaviour of a process up to that preorder; this is what is called a

characteristic formula

[
19
,
37
]
. In this
section we follow
[
4
]
in order to define such a formula for
processes with respect to
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
. We will consider the logic from
Section
4
with its greatest-fixed-point
interpretation given therein.

First, let us define the notion of characteristic formula for the particular case of the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-semantics.

Definition 13
.

A formula
\chi_{s}
is
characteristic
for
s
(with
respect to
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
) iff for all
i
it holds that
i\models\chi_{s}
if, and only if,
i\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}s
. (Note that this also
implies that
s\models\chi_{s}
.)

The next proposition states the explicit definition of characteristic formulae in the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
framework.

Proposition 15
.

The characteristic formula for a process
q
in a finite LTS can be obtained recursively as:

\chi_{q}=\bigwedge_{a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)}\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}\chi_{q^{\prime}}\wedge\bigwedge_{a!\in O}[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}\chi_{q^{\prime}},

(1)

that is, letting
D
be the declaration defined by the equations of the form (
1
), we
have that:
(\llparenthesis D\rrparenthesis_{\max},p)\models\chi_{q}
iff
p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
.

Remark 7
.

Note that
\chi_{q}
constrains the behaviour of a process that
satisfies it only for those input actions that
q
can perform,
exactly in the same way as the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
relation does.

In order to prove Proposition
15
, we
follow the general theory described in
[
4
]
. The concrete
goal is to apply Corollary 3.4 in
[
4
]
. As a first step, we
define
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
as the greatest fixed-point of a monotone function,
denoted by
\mathcal{F}_{io}
, over the set of binary relations over
S
, the
set of states of an LTS.

Definition 14
.

For each
R\subseteq S\times S
, we have that
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
iff

1.

\mathsf{ins}(q)\subseteq\mathsf{ins}(p)
,

2.

for all
a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)
, if
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
then there exists some
q^{\prime}
such that
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
and
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
, and

3.

for all
o!\in O
, if
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
then there exists some
q^{\prime}
such that
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle o!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
and
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
.

Next, we show a result connecting
\mathcal{F}_{io}
and
\chi
defined in
Proposition
15
.

Lemma 16
.

Let
R\subseteq S\times S
and
p,q\in S
. Consider the set of variables
\mathsf{Var}=\{X_{p}\mid p\in S\}
and let
\sigma_{R}
be the interpretation
defined as
\sigma_{R}(X_{q})=\{p\mid(p,q)\in R\}
. Then,
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
iff

(\sigma_{R},p)\models\bigwedge_{a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)}\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}\wedge\bigwedge_{a!\in O}[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}},

Proof.

First, suppose that
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
. We will show that

(\sigma_{R},p)\models\bigwedge_{a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)}\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}\wedge\bigwedge_{a!\in O}[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}.

To this end, let
a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)
. We show first that
(\sigma_{R},p)\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
. Indeed, since
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
,
\mathsf{ins}(q)\subseteq\mathsf{ins}(p)
. Hence,

a?\in\mathsf{ins}(p)
and therefore
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
. Assume now that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
, for some
p^{\prime}
, we claim that

(\sigma_{R},p^{\prime})\models\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
. Indeed, since
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
,
a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)
and

p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
, there exists some
q^{\prime}
such that
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
with
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
, that is, such that
p^{\prime}\in\sigma_{R}(X_{q^{\prime}})
. Thus, we have that
(\sigma_{R},p)\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
.

Next, let us see that
(\sigma_{R},p)\models\bigwedge_{a!\in O}[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
.
Analogously to the previous case let us assume that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
, for some
p^{\prime}
. We claim that

(\sigma_{R},p^{\prime})\models\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
. Indeed, since
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
and
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
,
there exists some
q^{\prime}
such that
q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
with
(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\in R
, that is, such that
p^{\prime}\in\sigma_{R}(X_{q^{\prime}})
.
Thus, we have that
(\sigma_{R},p)\models[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
. This completes the proof of
the first implication of the theorem.

Now, let us suppose that

(\sigma_{R},p)\models\bigwedge_{a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)}\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}\wedge\bigwedge_{a!\in O}[\,a!\,]\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}.

We prove that
(p,q)\in\mathcal{F}_{io}(R)
. First, since for each
a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)
,
(\sigma_{R},p)\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
, we obtain that
\mathsf{ins}(q)\subseteq\mathsf{ins}(p)
.

Next, let us consider some
a?\in\mathsf{ins}(q)
. Since
(\sigma_{R},p)\models\llbracket a?\rrbracket\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
, we have that
(\sigma_{R},p^{\prime})\models\bigvee_{q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}}X_{q^{\prime}}
for every
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
. Hence, for every
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
, there exists some
