f(p)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}f(p)
but
f(q)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
.

7.2
Applying modal decomposition

Assume that we want to know whether
f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\models\varphi
, with
f
specified by rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format and

\varphi\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. One could construct the LTS for
f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})
from those for
p_{1},\ldots,p_{n}
and then use the rules defining
the satisfaction relation
\models
to check whether
f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})
satisfies
\varphi
. However, as is well known, this approach
suffers from the so-called state-explosion problem. Alternatively, one
can apply a compositional approach: to check whether
f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\models\varphi
, one first constructs, from
\varphi
and the
rules defining the operation
f
, a collection of properties

\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{n}
such that
f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\models\varphi
if, and only if,
p_{i}\models\varphi_{i}
for

i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}
; after that, one checks whether each statement

p_{i}\models\varphi_{i}
holds. (Even though compositional model
checking suffers from the ‘formula-explosion problem’ in the worst
case and is therefore no panacea in general, variations on that
approach have been applied successfully in the literature—see, for
instance,
[
5
,
29
]
.)

Here we follow this compositional approach by applying the so-called
modal decomposition method of
[
10
,
17
]
. In those
papers, ruloids play an important role. For a GSOS languages, a ruloid

\frac{\raisebox{3.01385pt}{\normalsize{$\{x_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y_{ij}\mid 1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq m_{i}\}\cup\{x_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\mid 1\leq i\leq n,1\leq k\leq\ell_{i}\}$}}}{\raisebox{-4.30554pt}{\normalsize{$D[\vec{x}]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}C[\vec{x},\vec{y}]$}}}

(3)

where the
x_{i}
’s and the
y_{ij}
’s
(1\leq i\leq n
and
1\leq j\leq m_{i})
are all distinct variables,
m_{i}

and
l_{i}
are natural numbers,
D[\vec{x}]
is a term with variables including at most the
x_{i}
’s,

C[\vec{x},\vec{y}]
is a term with variables including at most the
x_{i}
’s and
y_{ij}
’s, and the
a_{ij}
’s,

b_{ik}
’s and
a
are actions from
L
.

In what follows,
we will limit ourselves to considering only operations in

\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format specified by rules whose target is either a variable or
a term of the form
g(z_{1},\ldots,z_{n})
, and the metavariables
t
and

u
will range over terms of those forms. The extension of our
results to arbitrary rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format can be carried out along
the lines in
[
10
,
17
]
.

In the light of our simplified setting,
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
rules, i.e., GSOS
rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format, play the role of ruloids
in
[
10
,
17
]
; the only other ruloids we need are those
for variables, viz.

\frac{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}}{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{right={\normalsize$a\in L$.}}

In what follows, we often refer to ruloids as rules.

A crucial result from
[
9
,
11
]
is that a transition

\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
is provable in a GSOS language if, and only if,
there exist a substitution
\sigma^{\prime}
and a GSOS ruloid
H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u

such that
\sigma^{\prime}
satisfies all premises in
H
(denoted by

\sigma^{\prime}\models H
),
\sigma^{\prime}(t)=\sigma(t)
and
\sigma^{\prime}(u)=p^{\prime}
.

Before defining the modal decomposition for
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
in general, let
us describe how to define it for the modal operator
\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle
, with

a?\in I
. First, recall that
p\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi
iff either

p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
or there exists some
p^{\prime}
such that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
with

p^{\prime}\models\varphi
. Hence, the modal decomposition must consider both
cases: when is possible to apply a rule that gives rise to an

a?
-transition, and when it is not. This first case is dealt with
following the definition of the decomposition of formulae in HML given
in
[
17
]
. For the second case, we first define the
following auxiliary notations and concepts.

Definition 17
.

Let
P
be a GSOS language,
t
a term, and
a?\in I
an input
action. We write
R(t,a?)
for the set of rules for
t
that emit

a?
. That is,

R(t,a?)=\{r\in P\mid\exists H,u.~r=H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u\}.

We write
H_{r}
for the set of premises of rule
r\in R(t,a?)
. Given a premise
\gamma\in H_{r}
and a
variable
x
, we define the formula
\mathsf{neg}(\gamma,x)\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
in the following way:

•

\mathsf{neg}(x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}},x)=\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
, with
b!\in O
.

•

\mathsf{neg}(x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime},x)=\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}
, with
b?\in I
.

•

\mathsf{neg}(y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}},x)=\mathsf{neg}(y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y^{\prime},x)=\mathbf{tt}
, with
y\neq x
.

Finally, we write
\chi(t,a?)
for the set of all the functions that pick a premise in
H_{r}
for each
r\in R(t,a?)
—that is,

\chi(t,a?)=\{\eta\mid\eta:R(t,a?)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to3.33333pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to3.33333pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to3.33333pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to3.33333pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\bigcup_{r\in R(t,a?)}{H_{r}},\,\text{such that $\eta(r)\in H_{r},\forall r\in R(t,a?)$}\}.

Remark 10
.

Intuitively,
\mathsf{neg}(\gamma,x)
gives us a
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
formula that
captures that the premise
\gamma
is not satisfied. For example, if

\gamma=x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, then
\mathsf{neg}(x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}},x)=\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}

and given a closed substitution
\sigma
, if
\sigma(x)\models\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
, then
\sigma(x)
does not satisfy the premise

\gamma
.

The following example illustrates the discussion in Remark
10
.

Example 16
.

Let us consider
t=f(x,y)
, with rules

\frac{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}}{f(x,y)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x}\quad\textsc{left=$r_{1}$}\and\frac{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}\quad y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y^{\prime}}{f(x,y)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{right={\normalsize.}, left=$r_{2}$}

We want to characterise when
\sigma(f(x,y))\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, for each closed substitution
\sigma
. In order to
do so, first let us define
\chi(f(x,y),a?)
. Since there are two premises in
H_{r_{2}}
,
\chi(f(x,y),a?)=\{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\}
, where:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\eta_{1}&=&\{r_{1}\mapsto x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}},r_{2}\mapsto x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}\}\,,\\
\eta_{2}&=&\{r_{1}\mapsto x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}},r_{2}\mapsto y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y^{\prime}\}\,.\end{array}

Now, let us define
\psi_{\eta_{i}}(z)=\bigwedge_{r\in R(t,a?)}\mathsf{neg}(\eta_{i}(r),z)
, for
z\in\{x,y\}
. That is,
working up to logical equivalence,

\begin{array}[]{rclcrcl}\psi_{\eta_{1}}(x)&=&\langle a!\rangle\mathbf{tt}\wedge\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}\,,&\psi_{\eta_{1}}(y)&=&\mathbf{tt}\,,\\
\psi_{\eta_{2}}(x)&=&\langle a!\rangle\mathbf{tt}\,,&\psi_{\eta_{2}}(y)&=&\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}\,.\\
\end{array}

We observe that if
\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta_{i}}(x)
and
\sigma(y)\models\psi_{\eta_{i}}(y)
for some
i\in\{1,2\}
, then
\sigma(f(x,y))\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, and that the converse implication also holds. (See
Proposition
26
in
B
for the formal result.)

We are now ready to define modal decomposition à la Fokkink and van
Glabbeek for the logic
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
. We will give the definition for an
alternative syntax of the logic
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
where, as noted in
Definition
3
, we use finitary conjunctions and
disjunctions, and follow the convention that an empty conjunction
stands for
\mathbf{tt}
and an empty disjunction stands for
\mathbf{ff}
.

Definition 18

(Modal decomposition)
.

The decomposition function

\cdot^{-1}:\mathbb{T}(\Sigma)\rightarrow(\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}\rightarrow\mathcal{P(\mathsf{Var}\rightarrow\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}})})

is defined in the following way:

•

\psi\in t^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
iff
\psi\in t^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)\cup t^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
where
t^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
and
t^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
are
defined as follows:

–

\psi\in t^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
iff exists a function
\eta\in\chi(t,a?)
such that

\psi=\psi_{\eta}
, where for each
x\in\mathsf{Var}
,

\psi_{\eta}(x)=\bigwedge_{r\in R(t,a?)}\mathsf{neg}(\eta(r),x).

–

\psi\in t^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
iff there are a rule
r=H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}t^{\prime}\in R(t,a?)
and a
decomposition mapping
\psi^{\prime}\in u^{-1}(\varphi)
such that, for each
x\in\mathsf{Var}
,

\psi(x)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H\\
y\in var(u)\end{subarray}}\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\psi^{\prime}(y)\wedge\psi^{\prime}(x)&\text{if $x\in var(u)$}\\
\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H\\
y\in var(u)\end{subarray}}\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\psi^{\prime}(y)&\text{if $x\notin var(u)$}\\
\end{array}\right.

•

\psi\in t^{-1}(\langle a!\rangle\varphi)
iff there exist some rule
{H}/{t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u}
and some decomposition mapping

\psi^{\prime}\in u^{-1}(\varphi)
such that, for each
x\in\mathsf{Var}
,

\psi(x)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l@{\hspace{0.2cm}}l}\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H\\

\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H\\
y\in var(u)\end{subarray}}\langle b!\rangle\psi^{\prime}(y)\wedge\bigwedge_{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 1.47002pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 1.47002pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 1.05pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.53339pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.53339pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-4.22227pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 1.05pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\in H}\langle\!|c?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}\hfil\hskip 5.69046pt&\text{if
$x\notin var(u)$}\\
\end{array}\right.

•

\psi\in t^{-1}(\bigvee_{i\in I}\varphi_{i})
iff
\psi\in\bigcup_{i\in I}t^{-1}(\varphi_{i})
(where
I
is a finite
index set).

•

\psi\in t^{-1}(\bigwedge_{i\in I}\varphi_{i})
iff there are
\psi_{i}\in t^{-1}(\varphi_{i})
for
i\in I
, such that
for all
x\in\mathsf{Var}
,
\psi(x)=\bigwedge_{i\in I}\psi_{i}(x)
, where
I
is finite.

Remark 11
.

The only positive premises that are considered when defining
\psi(x)
are those for which
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y

with
y\in var(u)
. Indeed, by the definition of GSOS rules, since all the variables are distinct, if

x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H
,
\psi(y)=\mathbf{tt}
if
y\notin var(u)
. Also, since
\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{tt}\equiv\mathbf{tt}
, the
last sentence means that the only positive premises
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y
that give a non-trivial
\psi(y)
are those
such that
y\in var(u)
.

Remark 12
.

Notice that
t^{-1}(\mathbf{ff})=\emptyset
(case
\bigvee_{i\in I}\varphi_{i}
for
I=\emptyset
) and
t^{-1}(\mathbf{tt})=\{\psi\}
,
where
\psi(x)=\mathbf{tt}
for all
x
(case
\bigwedge_{i\in I}\varphi_{i}
for
I=\emptyset
).

Our reader may wonder why the definition of
\psi\in t_{R}^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
is sufficient to ensure that

\sigma(t)\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi
whenever
\sigma(x)\models\psi(x)
for all
x\in var(t)
. Indeed, the definition in question
does not explicitly require that
\sigma(x)
satisfy the negative
premises in
H_{r}
. (Observe that this cannot be done because there is
no formula in
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}}
that is satisfied by all the processes that
cannot perform a
b!
-transition, for some output action
b!
.)
However, as the proof of Theorem
22
will make
clear (see
B
), since the rules are in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}

format, it isn’t necessary to express the negative premises in the
definition of
\psi\in t_{R}^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
. In fact, if

\sigma
does not satisfy any of the mappings
\psi^{\prime}\in t_{\chi}^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
, then
\sigma
satisfies all the
premises of at least one rule
r
in
R(t,a?)
and some mapping
\psi

associated to that rule contains all the information that is needed to
determine whether
\sigma(t)\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi
.

All this is formalised in Theorem
22
, but first we will show some examples of the use of
the modal decomposition.

Example 17
.

Let us consider the following rules in the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format (omitting the rules for the quiescence action):

\frac{}{g(y)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox and

. Let us calculate
f(x)^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})
. First, we have that

R(f(x),a?)=\{r_{2}\}
and, since
H_{r_{2}}
has two premises, there are two functions
\chi(t,a?)
, namely
\eta_{1}(r_{2})=x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y
and
\eta_{2}(r_{2})=x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
. Second, trivially,
g(y)^{-1}(\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})=\{\phi\}
, where

\phi(x)=\mathbf{tt}
for all
x
.

Now, let us write both
f(x)^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})
and
f(x)^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})
.

•

\psi_{\eta_{1}},\psi_{\eta_{2}}\in f(x)^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})
, where
\psi_{\eta_{1}}(x)=\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}
,
\psi_{\eta_{2}}(x)=\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
and
\psi_{\eta_{1}}(z)=\psi_{\eta_{2}}(z)=\mathbf{tt}
for all

z\neq x
.

•

\psi\in f(x)^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt})
iff
\psi(x)=\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{tt}\equiv\mathbf{tt}
and

\psi(z)=\mathbf{tt}
for all
z\neq x
.

This means that any process
p
is such that
f(p)\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle b!\rangle\mathbf{tt}
. Indeed, let us assume that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
and
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, in this case
f(p)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\sigma(g(y))
and we are done. On the other hand, if either
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
or
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, then
f(p)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
and therefore
f(p)
trivially satisfies the formula.

Theorem 22
.

Let
P
be a GSOS language in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format. For each term
t
, formula
\varphi
and closed substitution

\sigma
, we have
\sigma(t)\models\varphi
iff there exists
\psi\in t^{-1}(\varphi)
such that
\sigma(x)\models\psi(x)
, for all
x\in var(t)
.

Proof.

The proof of this result can be found in
B
.
∎

The next counter-example illustrates why the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format is needed in Theorem
22
.

Example 18
.

Let
f
be defined by the following rules, which are not in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format and are taken from
Example
11
on page
11
:

\frac{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox Applying the modal decomposition method to obtain
f(x)^{-1}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff})
, it is not hard to see that
\psi_{r_{1}}\in f(x)^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff})
, where
\psi_{r_{1}}(x)=\mathbf{tt}
.
The process
s
, with
s\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}s
, satisfies
\mathbf{tt}=\psi_{r_{1}}(x)
. On the other hand,

f(s)\not\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\mathbf{ff}
, which means that the
decomposition method is incorrect for this operation.

The rules for
f
do not meet clause
2a
in
Definition
16
because
a?
belongs to the positive
trigger for
x
in
r_{1}
but not to that for
x
in
r_{2}
. This clause
is essential in the proof of the “if implication” in
Theorem
22
.

7.3
A rule format for coherent quiescent behaviour

Operators for constructing LTSs with inputs and outputs should ensure ‘coherent quiescent behaviour’ in the
sense of Definition
1
. This means that each operator
f
, when applied to a vector of states

\vec{p}
in an LTS, should satisfy the following property:

f(\vec{p})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}\text{ iff }p^{\prime}=f(\vec{p})\text{ and, for each }a!\in O\setminus\{\delta!\},~f(\vec{p})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}.

(4)

In what follows, we will isolate sufficient conditions on the GSOS rules defining
f
that guarantee the
above-mentioned property.

Definition 19
.

We say that the following sets of formulae contradict each other:

•

\{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\}
and
\{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\}
for
a\in L
,

•

for
b!\in O\setminus\{\delta!\}
, and

•

H
and
H^{\prime}
when

and

are non-empty and
