.

Formulae
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y
and
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to6.66666pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
are said to negate each other.

We say that two sets of formulae
H_{1}
and
H_{2}
are contradictory if there are
H_{1}^{\prime}\subseteq H_{1}
and
H_{2}^{\prime}\subseteq H_{2}
such that
H_{1}^{\prime}
and
H_{2}^{\prime}
contradict each other.

Intuitively, two sets of contradictory formulae cannot be both satisfied by states in an LTS. For example, in the light of the
requirement on quiescent behaviour in Definition
1
, there is no state
p
in an LTS such that
p\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
for
each
b!\in O
. This observation motivates the third requirement in Definition
19
.

Definition 20
.

We say that an operation
f
is
quiescent consistent
if the set of rules for
f
satisfies the following two
constraints:

[
\delta_{1}
]

If
H/f(\vec{x})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}t
is a rule for
f
then

1.

for each
f
-defining rule
H^{\prime}/f(\vec{x})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}t^{\prime}
with
b!\in O\setminus\{\delta!\}
, the sets
H
and

H^{\prime}
are contradictory, and

2.

t=f(\vec{y})
for some vector of variables
\vec{y}
such that, for each index
i
, either
y_{i}=x_{i}
or

x_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y_{i}\in H
.

[
\delta_{2}
]

Let
\{r_{1},\ldots r_{n}\}
be the set of output-emitting rules for
f
not having

\delta!
as label of their conclusions.

Then the set of rules for
f
contains all rules of the form

\frac{\raisebox{3.01385pt}{\normalsize{$\{l_{1},\ldots,l_{n}\}$}}}{\raisebox{-4.30554pt}{\normalsize{$f(\vec{x})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}f(\vec{x})$}}},

where
l_{i}
negates some premise of
r_{i}
and no two sets of formulae included in
\{l_{1},\ldots l_{n}\}

contradict each other.

A GSOS language is
quiescent consistent
if so is each operation in it.

Theorem 23
.

If
f
is quiescent consistent then Property (
4
) holds for
f
.

Proof.

The proof of this result may be found in
C
.
∎

We end the section showing a negative result for the (binary) merge
operator of Example
4
. In that example, we mentioned
that, as noted in
[
6
]
, the merge of two systems need
not satisfy Property (
4
). Here we prove that there is no
extension of the
\delta!
-emitting rules for the merge operator with
rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format that leads to an operation affording
Property (
4
). The interested reader can find examples of
operators that are both quiescent and
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
conforming in the
paper
[
23
]
.

Proposition 24
.

There is no extension of the rules for the (binary) merge operator with a collection of
\delta!
-emitting rules
in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format that guarantees consistent quiescent behaviour.

Proof.

Our goal is to add
\delta!
-emitting rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format to those for the merge operator so that, for all
p,q

{p\wedge q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}r}\Leftrightarrow{r=p\wedge q\text{ and }p\wedge q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\forall a!\in O\setminus\{\delta!\}\;.}

(5)

We will show that this is impossible. Let us recall the output emitting rules for the binary merge operator:

\frac{x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}\quad y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y^{\prime}}{x\wedge y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}\wedge y^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{right={\normalsize$a!\in O$.}, left={$r_{a!}$}}

In particular,
r_{\delta}
ensures that
p\wedge q\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p\wedge q
for all quiescent
p
and
q
.

Now, assume that there is a set of
\delta!
-emitting rules
R
in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format such that
R\cup\{r_{a!}\mid a!\in O\}
ensures Property (
5
). Let us consider
a!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
and
b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
, with
a\neq b
. Since
a!\wedge b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
for each
c!\in O\setminus\{\delta!\}
there are some rule
r=H/x\wedge y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}t
and closed substitution
\sigma
such that:

(i)

\sigma(x)=a!
,
\sigma(y)=b!
;

(ii)

\sigma\models H
; and

(iii)

\sigma(t)=a!\wedge b!
.

Since
r
is in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format and
\sigma\models H
, its positive
premises can only have the form
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x^{\prime}
for some
x^{\prime}
and

y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y^{\prime}
for some
y^{\prime}
. The negative premises of
H
, if any,
have the form
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
or
y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
for some
c?\in I
. Moreover, as
a!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
,
b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
and

\sigma(t)=a!\wedge b!
, none of the variables
x^{\prime}
and
y^{\prime}
can occur
in
t
. So
t
can only have the form
x\wedge y
,
a!\wedge y
,

x\wedge b!
or
a!\wedge b!
. We claim that it must be
x\wedge y
.
Indeed, assume, by way of example, that
t=a!\wedge y
. Consider now
the substitution
\sigma^{\prime}(x)=a!+b!
and
\sigma^{\prime}(y)=b!
, where

a!+b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
and
a!+b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0
. It is straightforward to
check that
\sigma^{\prime}\models H
. Hence,
r
yields
(a!+b!)\wedge b!\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}a!\wedge b!
, which contradicts
Property (
5
). Similar examples can be constructed if

t=x\wedge b!
and
t=a!\wedge b!
.

Thus, we have
r=H/x\wedge y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}x\wedge y
. Consider now the substitution
\rho
such that

\rho(x)=\rho(y)=a!+b!
and
\rho(z)=0
for
z\notin\{x,y\}
. This substitution satisfies all the positive premises in
H
and, since all the negative
premises in
H
are of the form
y\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
for some
c?\in I
,

\rho
also satisfies those. Hence
\rho\models H
, and
\rho

together with
r
proves
(a!+b!)\wedge(a!+b!)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}(a!+b!)\wedge(a!+b!)
.

On the other hand, using
r_{a}
, we also have that
(a!+b!)\wedge(a!+b!)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}0\wedge 0
, which
contradicts Property (
5
).

It follows that no extension of the rules for the (binary) merge
operator with
\delta!
-emitting rules in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format satisfies
Property (
5
).
∎

8
Conclusion

In this paper, we have further developed the theory of

\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}

[
20
,
21
,
22
]
by studying logical
characterisations of this relation and its compositionality. We have
also compared the proposed logical characterisation of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
with
an existing logical characterisation for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}
proposed by Beohar
and Mousavi. The article also offers a precongruence rule format for

\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
and a rule format ensuring that operations take quiescence
properly into account. Both rule formats are based on the GSOS format
by Bloom, Istrail and Meyer.

We have provided a connection between the precongruence rule format
for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
and logical characterisations for that relation by
establishing a modal decomposition result, which yields a
compositional model-checking procedure for the studied logic with
respect to systems described using operations in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format. We
have also studied fixed-point extensions of the logics introduced in
this paper and offered a characteristic-formula construction for
processes modulo
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
.

In future research, it would be interesting to study whether the
precongruence rule format we provide in this paper can be made more general and
whether it can be derived from a modal decomposition result in the
style of Fokkink and van Glabbeek. Our modal decomposition result
relies heavily on properties of the
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
format; we believe
therefore that generalizing the precongruence rule format might be
difficult.

In this paper, we have considered the theory of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
as presented
in
[
20
,
21
,
22
,
23
]
. That theory does not consider the
internal action
\tau
, which is the source of many of the
complications in the classic
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}
testing theory. Extending the
theory of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
to a setting with the internal action
\tau
is an
interesting avenue for future research. As a first step, one could
develop a version of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
with the action
\tau\not\in L
, but
without abstracting from internal steps in system executions. This can
be done by requiring that condition
3
in the
definition of an
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-relation (Definition
2
) hold
also with respect to
\tau
-transitions—that is, that internal
transitions performed by implementations should be matched by
specifications as in the classic simulation preorder
[
34
]
. In
this framework, we could replay the theory developed in this paper. By
way of example, the rule format given in
Definition
16
can be extended to the resulting
version of
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
by requiring that
\tau
-emitting rules have only
input actions as labels of negative premises and output actions as
labels of positive premises. The resulting rule format would be
powerful enough to express the hiding operation considered
in
[
43
]
. Of course, the ultimate goal of extending
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}

with internal actions would be to develop a theory that abstracts from

\tau
like
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco}}
and that has some of the pleasing properties of
that classic notion of conformance, including a test generation
procedure. We consider the study of such a theory and of its potential
applications a worthy research goal for the future.

Appendix A
Proof of Theorem
21

Remark 13
.

All the substitutions we consider from now on map variables either to
variable-free terms or to states in some LTS.

Let
\mathcal{S}
be the least binary relation that includes

\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
and such that: if
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{i}
, for each
1\leq i\leq n
, and
f
is an
n
-ary operation in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format then

f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}f(q_{1},\ldots,q_{n})
.

The following property of
\mathcal{S}
will be useful in what follows.

Lemma 25
.

Let
t
be a term. Assume that
\sigma
and
\rho
are two
substitutions such that
\sigma(x)\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}\rho(x)
for
each variable
x
occurring in
t
. Then

\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}\rho(t)
.

Proof of Theorem
21
.

We show that
\mathcal{S}
is an
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
simulation by induction on the definition of
\mathcal{S}
. This is clear if
p\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q
because
p\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}q
. Assume
therefore that

p=f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}f(q_{1},\ldots,q_{n})=q

because
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{i}
for each
1\leq i\leq n
. We
proceed to prove that each of the defining conditions for
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
relations holds for
p
and
q
. In doing so, we shall use, as our
inductive hypothesis, the fact that those conditions hold for
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq n
).
In particular, we have that
\mathsf{ins}(q_{i})\subseteq\mathsf{ins}(p_{i})
(
1\leq i\leq n
).

•

We prove, first of all, that the set of initial input actions of

p
. To this end, assume that

.

To complete the proof for this case, we will now show how to use the

to prove the required transition

. To this end, we will construct a substitution

with the following properties:

for each

\sigma^{\prime}(z)\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}\rho(z)

The first condition above gives us that

by

. From the third, we obtain that

.

We can therefore set

•

Assume that
p=f(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p^{\prime}
for some

and state

. Then there are a rule

of the

) on page
2

\sigma(x_{i})=p_{i}
for each

–

p_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}{}

\sigma(C[\vec{x},\vec{y}])=p^{\prime}
.

Our goal is to use rule
r
to prove that
q=f(q_{1},\ldots,q_{n})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q^{\prime}
for some
q^{\prime}
such that
p^{\prime}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q^{\prime}
.

Since
a!
is an output action, condition
3
in
Definition
16
tell us that each
a_{ij}
is an output
action, and each
b_{ik}
is an input action. As each
a_{ij}
is an
output action,
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\sigma(y_{ij})
and
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq n
), the inductive
hypothesis yields that for each
i
and
j
there is some
q_{ij}

such that
q_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to16.66664pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a_{ij}!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}q_{ij}
and
\sigma(y_{ij})\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{ij}
. Note, moreover, that the
q_{i}
’s
satisfy the negative premises of rule
r
. Indeed,

p_{i}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to13.33331pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b_{ik}\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
(
1\leq i\leq n
and
1\leq k\leq\ell_{i}
)
and, as
p_{i}\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}q_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq n
), the inductive
hypothesis gives us that the set of input actions of each
q_{i}
is
included in that of
p_{i}
. Therefore, we have that rule
r

instantiated with a substitution
\rho
such that

\displaystyle\rho(x_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q_{i}\quad(1\leq i\leq n),\,\text{and}

\displaystyle\rho(y_{ij})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q_{ij}\quad(1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq m_{i})

yields the transition

q=f(q_{1},\ldots,q_{n})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a!$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\rho(C[\vec{x},\vec{y}]).

By construction,
\sigma(z)\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}\rho(z)
for each
variable
z
occurring in
C[\vec{x},\vec{y}]
.
Therefore
Lemma
25
now yields that

p^{\prime}=\sigma(C[\vec{x},\vec{y}])\mathbin{\mathcal{S}}\rho(C[\vec{x},\vec{y}]),

and we are done. ∎

Appendix B
Proof of Theorem
22

Before proving Theorem
22
, we will show two
auxiliary lemmas that will be use in its proof.

Lemma 26
.

Let
P
be a GSOS language in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format. For every term
t
, input

a?\in I
and closed substitution
\sigma
, we have that

\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
if, and only if, there exists some

\eta\in\chi(t,a?)
such that
\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta}(x)
for
each
x\in var(t)
.

Proof.

The result follows straightforwardly. First, recall that, by
definition,
\psi_{\eta}(x)=\bigwedge_{r\in R(t,a?)}\mathsf{neg}(\eta(r),x)
. Second, note that given a premise
\eta(r)\in H_{r}
, the formula
\mathsf{neg}(\eta(r),x)
is
such that
\sigma(x)\models\mathsf{neg}(\eta(r),x)
iff
\sigma(x)
does not
satisfy premise
\eta(r)
. So, if there is some
\eta\in\chi(t,a?)

such that
\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta}(x)
, for each
x\in var(t)
,
then no rule in
R(t,a?)
can be used to derive a transition from

\sigma(t)
. Conversely, if
\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, then, for each

r\in R(t,a?)
, there is some premise in
H_{r}
that is not satisfied
by
\sigma(t)
. Let
\eta
be the choice function that picks those
premises. Then, by the discussion above,

\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta}(x)
for each
x\in var(t)
.
∎

Lemma 27
.

Let
P
be a GSOS language in
\mathbin{\mathsf{ioco\underline{s}}}
-format. Let
r=H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u
and
r^{\prime}=H^{\prime}/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u
be such that if

x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H^{\prime}
with
y\in var(u)
, then
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.06668pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.06668pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.11108pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H
. Consider
\psi,\psi^{\prime}\in t^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)

and
\phi\in u^{-1}(\varphi)
such that
\psi
is obtained using ruloid
r
and
\phi
, and
\psi^{\prime}
is obtained
using ruloid
r^{\prime}
and
\phi
. Then
\psi(x)
implies
\psi^{\prime}(x)
for all
x\in var(t)
.

Proof.

We show that each conjunct of
\psi^{\prime}(x)
is implied by
\psi(x)
. Without loss of generality, let us assume

x\in var(u)
. By construction,

•

\psi(x)=\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H\\
y\in var(u)\end{subarray}}\langle\!|b?|\!\rangle\phi(y)\wedge\phi(x)
.

•

\psi^{\prime}(x)=\bigwedge_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to8.08337pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-2.3917pt\hbox to5.38892pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-2.3917pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-3.98611pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}z\in H^{\prime}\\
z\in var(u)\end{subarray}}\langle\!|c?|\!\rangle\phi(z)\wedge\phi(x)
.

By hypothesis, if
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}z\in H^{\prime}
with
z\in var(u)
, then
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}z\in H
. This implies that each
conjunct
\langle\!|c?|\!\rangle\phi(z)
of
\psi^{\prime}(x)
is also a conjunct of
\psi(x)
, and the claim follows.
∎

Proof of Theorem
22
.

The proof is by structural induction on the formula
\phi
. We only show the case of
\phi=\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi

since the others are the same as in
[
17
]
.

Only if implication
: Let us suppose that
\sigma(t)\models\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi
. Then, by definition of
the satisfaction relation
\models
, either
\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
or
there exists some
p
such that
\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
and

p\models\varphi
.

Let us first consider the case when
\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
. This means
that it is not possible to apply any of the rules with conclusion

t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u
for some
u
.
By Lemma
26
, there exist
\eta\in\chi(t,a?)

and
\psi_{\eta}
such that
\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta}(x)
for each

x\in var(t)
. That is, there exists some
\psi_{\eta}\in t^{-1}_{\chi}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
such that

\sigma(x)\models\psi_{\eta}(x)
, for all
x\in var(t)
, and we are done.

On the other hand, if there exists some
p
such that
\sigma(t)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}p
and
p\models\varphi
, there
are a rule
r=H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u
and a closed substitution
\sigma^{\prime}
such that
\sigma^{\prime}\models H
,

\sigma^{\prime}(t)=\sigma(t)
and
\sigma^{\prime}(u)=p
. Since
\sigma^{\prime}(u)\models\varphi
by the induction hypothesis
there exists some
\phi\in u^{-1}(\varphi)
such that
\sigma^{\prime}(z)\models\phi(z)
for all
z\in var(u)
. Now, let
us consider
\psi\in t^{-1}_{R}(\langle\!|a?|\!\rangle\varphi)
constructed as in Definition
18
from the
rule
r=H/t\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}u
and
\phi\in u^{-1}(\varphi)
.

In this case we have that
\sigma(x)=\sigma^{\prime}(x)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\sigma^{\prime}(y)

and
\sigma(x)=\sigma^{\prime}(x)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}
, for all
x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b?$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}y\in H
and

x\mathbin{\mathchoice{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle c!\kern 2.45836pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}{\mathrel{{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}\kern-3.00003pt\hbox to9.99998pt{$\leaders{\hbox{\kern-3.00003pt\hbox{$\mathord{-}$}}}{\hfill}$}\kern-5.0pt\hbox{$\mathord{\not\rightarrow}$}}}\limits^{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle c!\kern 2.20837pt$}\kern-3.0pt}}}}}}}\in H
. We show that
\sigma(x)=\sigma^{\prime}(x)\models\psi(x)
